## 二、数据集描述（平台原文）

> ⚠️ 平台目录描述存在**错配**（已实测：insiders1 / imbalance5 的描述指向无关数据集）。以「五、全字段明细」的真实字段语义为准。

This dataset provides comprehensive, standardized global company fundamental data, covering 96% of global market capitalization. It includes annual and interim/quarterly financial statements, per share data, calculated ratios, pricing, and textual information for over 1500 data items. The data is point-in-time, capturing values as they were known historically, and supports peer comparability across regions and industries. With detailed income statements, balance sheets, cash flows, and supplemental metrics, this dataset enables robust analysis of company financial health, valuation, profitability, and risk. It is highly valuable for quantitative research, backtesting, and building predictive models to forecast price movements based on changes in fundamentals, financial ratios, and corporat
